================================================================
  CRYPTO LAYER — TETA-Guard Pre-Detection Filter
  Algorithm 3  (Eqs. 3.15 HMAC + 3.16 Freshness + 3.17 Nonce)
  Input  : outputs/PEM_EVENT_LOG/12_ME_S4_Malicious_Controller_With_RSU.csv
  Modules: KEM (§3) + HMAC (§4) + ThresholdSig (§5)
           LocationBinding (§6) + LKH (§7)
================================================================

  Architectural rule (§3.4.9):
    ✓  Vehicle/RSU-origin attacks → DROPPED here (never reach TGN)
    ✗  Controller-origin attacks  → ACCEPTED here (TGN is primary defense)

  Per-event format:
    STEP ①  KEM lookup + revocation check
    STEP ②  HMAC-SHA256 integrity  (Eq. 3.15)
    STEP ③  Timestamp freshness    (Eq. 3.16)
    STEP ④  Nonce novelty           (Eq. 3.17)
    VERDICT: CRYPTO_ACCEPT → TGN  |  CRYPTO_DROP_* → silent drop

================================================================

  ─── PLACEMENT DECISION MATRIX (Section 10) ───────────────
  TTW  vehicle/RSU-origin : BLOCKED here (Eq. 3.16 freshness)
  BSHH vehicle/RSU-origin : BLOCKED here (Eq. 3.17 nonce)
